  application-status-writer-cert-hook (R2-D + WF-2)
    call signature contract (APPROVED)
      ✓ passes applicationId, actorId, and the caller client (no skipInitialAssets — hook owns initial assets per C-1) (95 ms)
      ✓ probes findCertificateForApplication BEFORE generateCertificate (72 ms)
    idempotency
      ✓ re-call returns existing cert via findCertificateForApplication probe (79 ms)
      ✓ H-1: emits CERT_AUTO_GEN_SKIPPED_EXISTING via onAudit when cert exists (15 ms)
      ✓ double-write of the same APPROVED transition is safe (81 ms)
    rollback on failure (reverts APPROVED → fromStatus)
      ✓ cert-gen throw rolls status back to fromStatus and re-throws wrapped (114 ms)
      ✓ rollback failure is swallowed (logged), original cert error still re-thrown (85 ms)
      ✓ rollback uses prisma.application.update directly (no recursive writeApplicationStatus) (82 ms)
    opt-out via autoIssueCertificate: false
      ✓ hook does not fire when explicitly opted out (89 ms)
      ✓ default true means hook fires on APPROVED with no explicit flag (80 ms)
    cert hook fires on APPROVED only — never on AUDIT_PASSED (ISO/IEC 17065 §7.6)
      ✓ AUDIT_PASSED does NOT issue a certificate — passing the audit is not the decision (335 ms)
